# Repository drift audit

Scope: every repository file present in the working tree, reviewed against `2c0794eb^..HEAD` and the current uncommitted stabilization changes.

Legend: unchecked = pending; checked = reviewed. Notes record only material findings, invariants, or generator-based evidence.

## Findings

- Required runtime-server tests now fail fast when their executable or resources are absent; they no longer pass by assumption.
- Native JavaFX shared-contract coverage is restored on Windows and macOS, and the in-process WebDriver test is restored on macOS.
- Scalar, navigation, paint, and resize assertions were consolidated into the stronger shared contract; four subset smoke/matrix classes were removed.
- The stabilization-only `CefTestLifecycle` policy collapsed to unconditional shutdown and was removed without removing any termination calls.
- No test retries, `continue-on-error`, or failure-masking workflow paths were found. Retries remain limited to dependency/archive acquisition.
- `// XXX` marks the two unresolved native exceptions: CEF 109/116 macOS Swing coverage and Windows Swing message-loop shutdown.
- Generated CEF/CDP documentation is preserved. Generated families were reviewed through their schemas, emitters, deterministic regeneration, compilation, and schema tests.
- CDP Java output now uses the shared codegen banner and `@Generated` annotation infrastructure.
- CDP codegen parses Chromium PDL directly into a derived uPickle model; Python and Jackson are not codegen prerequisites.
- Scala codegen is braced Scala 3 throughout and now fails compilation on unused code, discarded values, non-unit statements, deprecations, and other warnings.
- Scala codegen uses immutable collection transformations throughout; the PDL grammar folds immutable parser state and matches whitespace-normalised declarations with Scala 3 string extractors.
- Java NIO directory streams are closed centrally, generated writes are atomic, and unsafe production `Option.get` calls were removed.
- Codegen comments now retain only protocol, ABI, ownership, compatibility, and generated-API constraints; stale plans and code narration were removed.
- CI anchors pin repeated CEF values while job-level environment removes per-lane boilerplate; the shared build entry point remains reusable by release CI and local hosts.
- Xvfb validation exposed and fixed a reactor-staging regression: the runtime packager now stages the JNI bridge as well as CEF resources, with CLI coverage.
- Stale handler-return documentation claimed the test returned `true` while it has always returned `false`; the narrative was removed.
- The removed reactor-native system property left an unreachable loader branch and test-only wrapper in `SystemBootstrap`; both were folded into the live classpath/external-runtime path.
